Multilingual evaluation rarely asks: it compares final answers and discards the actions. Yet those actions are the product: they fix cost and latency, decide how the system fails, and are the only auditable part of its behaviour. We make the action policy the measured object across 8 models, 6 parallel benchmarks and 41 languages (2.38M rollouts). The naive measurement fails: five confounds","starred":true,"priority":"p1","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T16:18:34Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11110.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个此前几乎没被正面测量的问题：多语言 tool-using agent 在语义等价任务上，跨语言后是否还保留同一套行动策略，而不是只看最终答案是否一致。对 agent 来说，真正决定成本、时延、失败模式和可审计性的，是中间 action trace，不是最后一句答案。难点不在收集轨迹，而在如何把“跨语言策略差异”从长度偏置、空轨迹、随机重合、解码噪声和模型自身不稳定性里分离出来，得到可解释的 retention 指标。","importance":"虽然它不属于狭义 pretrain 论文，但对基座研究有两层价值。第一，它把 multilingual 能力从 answer parity 推进到 policy retention，这更接近真实部署中的行为一致性，也更能反推预训练与 post-train 是否学到了语言无关的工具使用策略。第二，作者发现多个差异很大的模型在 greedy 条件下都收敛到接近的 retention 水平，大约只保留 71–73% 的原有行动策略；这说明跨语言迁移里存在稳定的 policy loss，而不是个别模型的偶然缺陷。对预训练研究者来说，这会直接引出两个问题：训练语料中的英语中心性是否塑造了 agent policy；以及 instruction tuning / tool-use SFT 是否把非英语请求隐式路由成英语中间表示。","technical_approach":"技术核心是一个校正后的跨语言 action-policy 测量协议。作者在固定 scaffold 下，让模型在共享的五工具字母表中执行任务，并显式输出已执行的 action trace。数据覆盖 8 个模型、6 个平行 benchmark、41 种语言，总计 2.38M rollouts。若直接比较不同语言的 trace 相似度，会被五类混杂因素污染：短轨迹天然更容易相似；空轨迹会虚高；无关轨迹也可能有较高 chance overlap；模型在同语言重复运行时本就不完全一致；没有 same-language baseline 时，跨语言差异不可识别。\n\n作者的修正办法有几个关键点。第一，每个语言-任务单元都在相同解码设置下跑两次，只改变 serving seed。第二，定义 same-language agreement I_within：同一语言两次运行的轨迹一致性；定义 cross-language agreement I_cross：不同语言、不同 seed 的轨迹一致性。第三，用归一化保留率 \\tilde{I}=I_cross/I_within，把跨语言一致性除以模型自身可复现性上限，得到“语言变化后保留了多少原有策略”。第四，所有比较都做双向 length matching，并丢弃任一侧为空轨迹的样本，避免长度和空轨迹伪信号。第五，用 task-level bootstrap 给区间估计。\n\n在机制分析上，作者进一步测试为什么会丢 policy retention。证据指向英语 pivot：Translate 是最常用工具，且即便输入是天城文等非拉丁脚本，模型的 reasoning text 也约 99% 为 ASCII。去掉 translation tool 会按模型原本对翻译工具的依赖程度降低 length-matched agreement；强制使用 translation tool 则按预注册顺序帮助不同模型。也就是说，很多模型不是直接在目标语言上执行同一策略，而是先把问题映射到英语工作流，再在英语中完成规划与工具调用。","experiments":"实验规模很大：8 个模型、6 个平行 benchmark、41 种语言、2.38M agent rollouts。核心结果有三组。第一，在 greedy decoding 下，全部 24 个主要 cell 都出现正的跨语言 policy gap，且 raw gap 的 bootstrap 区间都排除了 0，说明差异稳定存在。第二，做完全部校正后，效应不是变小而是变大；这很关键，因为它说明过去看不见问题，不是因为问题弱，而是因为测量方法把它淹没了。第三，在四个差异很大的模型上，归一化 policy retention 在 greedy 条件下高度接近，约 71–73%；并且这种规律不只体现在模型均值上，而是体现在 24 个 cell 的分布上，模型身份只能解释约 5.7% 的方差。\n\n温度实验也很有信息量。作者做了五档 temperature ladder，发现随着采样温度升高，cross-language agreement 变化不大，但 same-language self-consistency 明显下降，因此如果只看未经归一化的相似度，采样噪声会掩盖真实的跨语言 policy loss。机制实验方面，Translate 工具在所有适配 benchmark 中都是最高频工具；reasoning 文本几乎全是 ASCII，说明非英语任务常被转写到英语内部通道。移除翻译工具会按模型原有翻译使用率成比例地降低 agreement；强制翻译则按预注册预测顺序改善结果。若精读全文，建议重点核对各 benchmark 的工具定义是否足够统一、length matching 的具体算法、以及不同语言族上的 retention 是否存在系统性偏差。","reading_guide":"这篇值得精读。先看 Introduction 和方法部分，弄清作者为什么坚持把 action trace 而不是 final answer 作为被测对象，以及五个 confound 分别如何扭曲结论。随后重点看定义 I_within、I_cross 和 \\tilde{I} 的章节，这里是全文最重要的识别设计。再看 Figure 2 一类主结果图，确认“所有校正都会让效应更大”这一点。之后读温度实验和 translation-tool 机制分析，这两部分解释了为什么多语言 agent 看起来能答对，但内部 policy 仍然不一致。\n\n如果要带着预训练视角去读，建议特别关注两点。第一，英语 pivot 现象是否来自预训练语料分布、tokenizer、还是 post-train 工具使用数据的英语中心性。第二，71–73% 这种近似常数是否暗示了某种跨语言 policy bottleneck，类似于“语言变了，但内部 planner 只保留部分可迁移结构”。预计完整读一遍正文加关键附录需要 45–60 分钟。附录里关于 estimator、confound cost 和 design checks 的部分不要跳过，否则很容易低估这个测量问题的难度。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper measures something multilingual evaluation has mostly ignored: whether a tool-using agent preserves the same action policy when the task is expressed in another language. Final-answer parity is not enough for agents, because the route determines cost, latency, failure mode, and what can actually be audited or governed. The hard part is not collecting traces but identifying a clean cross-lingual policy signal despite length bias, empty traces, chance overlap, decoding noise, and the model’s own within-language instability.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it matters for foundation-model research in two ways. First, it upgrades multilingual evaluation from answer equivalence to policy retention, which is much closer to real deployment behavior and more informative about whether the model has learned language-invariant tool-use structure. Second, the paper finds a striking regularity: under greedy decoding, several very different models retain only about 71–73% of their own action policy after a language change. That suggests a stable cross-lingual policy loss rather than an idiosyncratic failure of one model. For pretraining researchers, this raises direct questions about English-centric data mixtures, internal pivot languages, and whether tool-use post-training teaches non-English requests to route through English latent workflows.","technical_approach":"$1d","experiments":"$1e","reading_guide":"$1f","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇会直接改你做 multilingual agent eval 的 protocol；先看五个 confound 的拆解，再看 greedy 下仍不保策略这一条，值得内部 benchmark 立刻照抄。","en":"This should change how you run multilingual agent evals; read the five confounds first, then the result that policy divergence survives greedy decoding, and copy the protocol into your internal benchmark."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft Research India"],"authors":["Sourabrata Mukherjee","Kalika Bali","Sunayana Sitaram"],"notable_authors":["Kalika Bali","Sunayana Sitaram"],"tags":["multilingual-agents","evaluation-protocol","tool-use","action-traces","policy-retention"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11110","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.11110","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.10665","title":"VERDICT: Training-Free Step-Wise Verification of Multimodal Reasoning via Disagreement-Aware Consensus","abstract":"Multimodal large language models often generate reasoning chains containing subtle errors that lead to incorrect answers. Current verification approaches have notable limitations. Existing approaches either require expensive labelled supervision with inconsistent cross-task performance or aggregate scores from multiple sources by simple aggregations, missing a key insight: when these scores disagree, that disagreement itself carries important information about whether a reasoning step is truly v","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T08:46:38Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10665.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.CV","cs.GT"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文解决的是多模态推理链的“逐步验证”问题：MLLM 生成的中间步骤常带有细小但致命的视觉/逻辑错误，导致最终答案错。现有做法要么依赖训练出来的 critic/PRM（标注贵、跨任务不稳），要么用多个打分源做简单聚合（平均/投票），但忽略了“分歧本身就是信号”。作者要做的是：在不训练、不开新标注的前提下，把多源 verifier 的分歧结构显式建模成可用的 step-level 选择准则。","importance":"对预训练本身关联较弱，更偏 post-train/推理时验证与搜索；但它对“训练信号质量”和“用冻结评审器组合出更稳的监督/筛选信号”有启发：如果要做过程监督数据合成或 PRM 训练，VERDICT 这种分歧感知的共识分数可以作为更干净的伪标签/过滤器，减少跨任务退化。另一个价值是工程可落地：不训练、闭式解、可插拔，适合快速做 ablation 或作为强基线。","technical_approach":"VERDICT 把 step-wise verification 写成“耦合打分”问题，而不是把多个分数做独立聚合。\n- 多视角冻结评审：三个冻结 judge/agent 分别从 Visual、Logical、Contextual 角度给每个候选推理步骤打分。\n- 分歧感知共识：作者把这些分数的相互影响建模为一个协调博弈（coordination game），并声称存在唯一的闭式 Nash equilibrium。共识计算把原始分数变成“共识调整后的分数”：每个 judge 的最终立场会考虑其他 judge 的意见，因此不仅看均值，也看谁在和谁冲突、冲突是否可被协调。\n- 双重接受准则：先做 mean confidence check（整体认可度要够），再做 dispersion check（跨 judge 的一致性要够）。通过这两道门的候选步骤才被接受；在接受集合内再按共识置信度排序，选最稳定的 continuation。\n- 关键对比点：平均/方差过滤是对称且丢结构的；VERDICT 的耦合使“固执的视觉 judge”与“易被说服的上下文 judge”产生不对称影响，这类模式用固定权重或简单统计量很难复现。","experiments":"作者在 6 个 benchmark（覆盖空间推理、视觉 grounding、多模态抽象）上评估，报告相对基线最高提升 +5.95%，并强调一个性质：在所有测试 benchmark 上都不低于 baseline（无退化），而若干需要训练数据的 domain-specific critic 在跨任务上会出现明显波动甚至负增益。论文摘要与引言未给出各基线名称对应的逐项数字表；需要在实验章节核对：\n- 使用的 base MLLM 与生成候选步骤的策略（beam/采样/多候选数 K）。\n- 三个 judge 的具体实现（是否同一个模型不同 prompt，或不同模型）。\n- dispersion/mean 阈值如何设定、是否需要调参。\n这些细节决定它能否作为“训练数据过滤器”迁移到别的任务。","reading_guide":"泛读目标是抓住“分歧=信号”如何落到闭式共识与筛选规则上：\n- 先看方法章节里共识的闭式解推导与博弈解释：这是 VERDICT 与平均/投票的本质差异。\n- 再看 acceptance criterion（mean + dispersion）与 ranking 规则：这是它保证“不退化”的主要来源。\n- 最后看实验里跨任务稳定性的表格：重点不是单点提升，而是哪些 critic 会退化、VERDICT 为什么不退化。\n预计 40–60 分钟可完成有效泛读；若要用于数据合成/PRM 伪标签，建议额外精读 judge 设计与阈值敏感性消融。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets step-wise verification for multimodal reasoning chains: MLLMs often produce locally plausible intermediate steps that contain subtle visual or logical errors, which then cascade into wrong final answers. Prior approaches either rely on trained critics/PRMs (expensive supervision, unstable transfer) or use simple score aggregation across frozen sources (mean/vote), which discards the information carried by disagreement patterns. The goal is a training-free verifier that explicitly uses disagreement as a first-class signal.","importance":"It is not a pretraining contribution; it sits in inference-time verification and post-training tooling. The pretraining-adjacent value is in signal construction: if one wants to synthesize process-supervision data or train PRMs, a disagreement-aware consensus score can serve as a cleaner pseudo-label/filter than naive averaging, potentially reducing cross-task regressions. It is also an engineering-friendly baseline: plug-in, no training, closed-form computation.","technical_approach":"$20","experiments":"Evaluated on six benchmarks spanning spatial reasoning, visual grounding, and multimodal abstraction. They report improvements up to +5.95% over baselines and emphasize a robustness property: VERDICT never drops below the baseline on any tested benchmark, whereas trained domain-specific critics can improve some tasks but degrade others. The excerpt does not include the full experimental tables; to assess reproducibility and transfer, the key missing details to verify in the main text are: which base MLLMs are used, how many candidate steps/continuations are generated (K) and by what decoding strategy, how the three judges are instantiated (same model with different prompts vs different models), and how sensitive the mean/dispersion thresholds are.","reading_guide":"Skim with three checkpoints:\n1) The closed-form consensus derivation and the coordination-game interpretation (this is the core novelty vs ad hoc aggregation).\n2) The acceptance criteria (mean + dispersion) and the ranking rule (this largely explains the “no degradation” claim).\n3) The cross-benchmark stability tables: focus on where trained critics regress and whether VERDICT avoids those regimes.\nA solid skim takes ~40–60 minutes; if you plan to use it for data filtering or pseudo-labeling, also read the judge design and threshold sensitivity ablations carefully.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"将多个 verifier 之间的分歧（disagreement）形式化为博弈论问题，作为无需训练的验证信号，对构建高质量的 RL 奖励模型或合成数据有启发。","en":"Formalizes disagreement among multiple verifiers as a game-theoretic problem to serve as a training-free verification signal, offering insights for building high-quality RL reward models or synthetic data."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Microsoft Research"],"authors":["Rohit Sinha","Kunal Tilaganji","Tanuja Ganu","Nagarajan Natarajan","Amit Sharma","Vineeth Balasubramanian"],"notable_authors":["Amit Sharma"],"tags":["Multimodal Reasoning","Step-wise Verification","Training-Free","Game Theory"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10665","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.10665","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.10315","title":"Is This Your Final Answer? Cross-Contextual Consistency as a Measure of LLM Credibility","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) are powerful black-box systems, making it difficult to discern whether their answers reflect stable internal beliefs or superficial pattern matching. We identify cross-contextual consistency as an underutilized behavioral property of LLMs: a credible answer should remain stable when the same task is placed under topic-aligned, content-neutral contextual variation. Building on this intuition, we operationalize Cross-Contextual Consistency (C3) by comparing model gener","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-10T23:38:10Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10315.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作想回答的不是“模型答对了多少”，而是“模型给出的答案是否像一个稳定的内部判断”。作者把这个问题具体化为 cross-contextual consistency：如果任务语义不变，只是换了与主题相关但内容中性的上下文包装，一个可信的答案不应大幅漂移。它要解决的是现有置信度评估的两个缺口：一类方法依赖 token probability 或内部状态，闭源模型上不可用；另一类方法如 self-report、self-consistency、paraphrase consistency 容易被过度自信、错误答案自洽、语义改写失真等问题污染。作者因此提出一个纯黑盒、实例级的行为度量，用上下文扰动前后的输出分布差异来估计答案可信度。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接作用有限，它更像评测与模型行为分析工作；但它有两个值得记住的点。第一，它把“稳定性”从传统 paraphrase robustness 推进到更接近交叉盘问的设定：不是简单换说法，而是在任务语义不变的前提下改变 surrounding context。第二，它提供了一个不依赖 logits、可用于闭源模型、还能覆盖开放式生成任务的可靠性信号。对基座研究者来说，这类信号可作为 post-train 或 continual evaluation 的补充指标，尤其适合在 benchmark 饱和、准确率区分度下降时识别脆弱样本、污染样本和未学会区域。","technical_approach":"方法名是 C3。核心流程是：对同一个任务实例构造一组 topic-aligned、content-neutral 的上下文扰动版本；分别采样模型在原始提示和扰动提示下的输出；再计算两组输出分布之间的差异，把差异大小作为该实例的 cross-contextual consistency 分数。分数越稳定，作者观察到越可能正确或事实可靠。论文的一个优点是它没有把 C3 绑定到单一任务形式，而是针对多选、短答、长事实生成、代码生成等不同输出空间设计了可比较的分布差异估计方式。理论动机部分借用了逻辑语义学的语言，把模型内部知识看作一组命题及其诱导的世界集合，直觉上如果模型对某个命题有稳定支持，那么在语义等价或任务无关的上下文变化下，其输出分布不应显著改变。这个理论框架并不严格刻画真实 LLM 内部机制，但足以解释为什么“上下文扰动下的分布漂移”可以作为 credibility proxy。和 self-consistency 的区别在于，self-consistency 测的是同一 prompt 下多次采样是否收敛；C3 测的是跨 prompt 但保留任务语义时是否稳定。和 paraphrase consistency 的区别在于，作者强调 perturbation 应是内容中性的 contextual variation，而不是直接改写问题本身，以减少语义漂移带来的假不一致。","experiments":"实验覆盖 26 个模型、6 个 benchmark，主分析集是 16 个模型，另有 10 个模型做补充案例。任务范围包括 reasoning、factuality 和 code generation。主要结论有三层：一是实例层面，较小的 cross-contextual shift 与更高正确率/事实性显著相关；二是模型层面，C3 能形成一种 robustness profile，用来区分哪些模型在某些 benchmark 上只是“会做题”，哪些模型在语义不变扰动下仍保持稳定；三是方法层面，这种相关性对不同扰动来源和不同分布差异估计器都较稳健。作者还强调 C3 在 benchmark 饱和或 contamination 较重时仍有诊断价值，因为它不直接依赖标准答案打分，而是看行为稳定性。若精读实验，建议重点核对：不同任务上 C3 与 accuracy/factuality 的相关系数；闭源与开源模型上是否同样成立；错误样本中哪些类型最容易出现高漂移；以及与 self-consistency、paraphrase consistency、token-probability calibration 等基线相比的增益。当前给出的全文片段没有完整表格，无法安全复述具体数值。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读。先看引言和 motivation，确认作者想测的是 credibility 而不是 correctness。然后看 C3 的定义和不同任务上的 estimator 设计，这是方法是否站得住的关键。实验部分优先看三块：实例级相关性、模型级 robustness profile、对 perturbation source 和 comparison estimator 的稳健性。理论部分可以快速读，它主要提供直觉框架，不是严格证明。预计 25–35 分钟能抓住主要贡献。如果只想判断是否值得纳入评测栈，读方法定义、主结果表和案例分析就够了。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not asking how often an LLM is correct. It asks a different question: when a model gives an answer, does that answer behave like a stable internal commitment or like a brittle surface pattern? The proposed lens is cross-contextual consistency. If the task-relevant meaning stays fixed and only the surrounding context is varied in a topic-aligned but content-neutral way, a credible answer should not shift much. This addresses two gaps in existing confidence estimation. Internal-signal methods need logits or hidden states and are unusable for many closed models. Simpler behavioral methods such as self-report, self-consistency, or paraphrase consistency can be confounded by overconfidence, repeated wrong answers, or semantic drift in the prompt rewrite.","importance":"Its direct relevance to pretraining is modest; this is primarily an evaluation and behavioral analysis paper. First, it sharpens the notion of robustness beyond ordinary paraphrase invariance by focusing on contextual cross-examination rather than mere rewording. Second, it offers a black-box metric that works for open-ended generation and closed APIs, which makes it practical for model monitoring, post-training evaluation, and benchmark regimes where plain accuracy is becoming saturated or contaminated.","technical_approach":"$21","experiments":"$22","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. an","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"huggingface","sources":["huggingface"],"published_at":"2026-08-12T00:00:00.000Z","category_id":"eval","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11878.png","hf_upvotes":1,"arxiv_categories":[],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文关注工具型 安全问题从“手工写几个环境和攻击样例”推进到“自动合成有状态环境、自动找注入点、自动生成任务和验证结果”。对预训练研究员的启发主要在两个接口：一是安全训练数据的可扩展生成，二是工具使用能力与安全约束之间的冲突如何被评测和训练信号捕捉。它也提示了一个常被低估的问题：模型是否遵循系统指令，不只取决于模型内部能力，还取决于环境观测在轨迹中的位置和呈现方式。","technical_approach":"ToolHazard 由三个模块组成。Environment Simulator 用 LLM 驱动生成可执行、有状态的工具交互环境，环境被形式化为实体与状态变量、工具 API、状态转移规则三部分，避免只用随机 LLM 模拟工具返回带来的不可复现问题。Attacker Agent 自动发现 attacker-writable 且会传播到 Agent 观测中的状态位置，再生成与环境语义匹配的注入 应用域细节，保留对“可验证安全数据合成”的方法印象。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies indirect prompt injection for tool-using LLM agents. The attacker does not modify the user request, system prompt, tool code, or model parameters; instead, malicious instructions are written into environment states such as emails, documents, database fields, or tool outputs. The hard part is scaling the construction of executable, stateful, reproducible, and verifiable adversarial environments, rather than crafting a few isolated attack strings.","importance":"The contribution is not about foundation-model pretraining directly, but it is relevant to agent post-training, safety alignment, and scalable training-data generation. ToolHazard turns agent security evaluation from manually built sandboxes into a pipeline that can synthesize environments, discover injection points, create long-horizon tasks, and verify unsafe outcomes programmatically. For pretraining-oriented researchers, the useful signal is at the pretrain–posttrain interface: tool-use competence and safety constraints need environment-grounded training signals, and robustness depends strongly on how observations are encountered during a trajectory.","technical_approach":"ToolHazard has three main components. The Environment Simulator generates executable stateful tool-interactive environments, represented by structured entities and state variables, tool APIs, and transition rules. This avoids the nondeterminism of pure LLM-based tool simulation and makes evaluation reproducible. The Attacker Agent searches for writable environment states that can propagate into the agent’s observations, then creates payloads tailored to the environment and the available tools. This differs from prior red-teaming setups that assume predefined injection locations. The User Simulator creates state-grounded long-horizon tasks, so the task set grows with the environment pool. The same framework is also used to synthesize adversarial alignment data for supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning.","experiments":"The authors build ToolHazard-Bench with 87 long-horizon tasks, 28 stateful environments, and 512 tools. The benchmark is designed to have more complex workflows than earlier hand-built agent-security benchmarks such as ASB and AgentDojo. Experiments show that representative LLM agents remain vulnerable to environment-side prompt injections. Two empirical patterns are especially useful: attacks work better when the injected instruction is encountered earlier in the execution trajectory, and when it appears near the end of an observation. Alignment with ToolHazard-generated data improves robustness on both ToolHazard-Bench and AgentDojo while preserving benign task utility. The provided text does not include full numerical tables, so exact gain values should be checked in the experimental section of the paper.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Only 2-6 tokens are needed per reasoning step, drastically reducing decoding length. XCoT supervision is automatically constructed offline from logged driving trajectories paired with causal scene semantics. During inference, the model first generates the XCoT sequence, which conditions trajectory generation via shared multimodal self-attention. Deterministic token routing sends XCoT tokens to a dedicated Reason FFN, and trajectory queries to a separate Control FFN, followed by a flow matching head to output continuous future trajectories. The accompanying XCPO algorithm refines the reasoning policy using trajectory-level rewards while keeping the action executor frozen.","experiments":"Evaluations cover in-distribution planning, lane change scenarios, inference latency, and training stability. As a result, verb-grounding information present in trajectories is systematically degraded during discretization and compression, and downstream language-conditioned policy learning has to operate on an already distorted action vocabulary. adding reconstruction encoders, geometry adapters, or other inference-time spatial modules.","importance":"The main relevance to foundation-model pretraining is the representation interface. Space Tokens treat reserved token positions as carriers of continuous latent states, supervised by external geometric signals during training and used as native tokens at inference. This connects to visual tokenization, latent knowledge tokens, and multimodal mid-training: a teacher model supplies structured non-text information, while the deployed VLM keeps the same autoregressive interface. The contribution is methodological rather than a large-scale pretraining recipe, but it is useful for thinking about how non-linguistic structure can be folded into an LM token stream.","technical_approach":"Space Tokens reserve a subset of the vocabulary and let the VLM generate these tokens just like normal autoregressive tokens. Their discrete identities are not the main representation; the final-layer hidden states at these positions are used as continuous spatial embeddings. The training supervision has two branches. One distills scene-level 3D geometry from large 3D reconstruction models into the VLM latent space. The other encodes object-centric 3D bounding-box attributes, including position, orientation, and size, into dedicated spatial tokens. Compared with attaching depth, point-cloud, or geometry encoders, the heavy geometry model is only used during training. The trade-off is that the learned latent tokens depend on teacher quality and need decoders or probes for explicit verification.","experiments":"The evaluation covers spatial reasoning, visual QA, and general VQA. The more useful evidence is qualitative and diagnostic: the 3D teacher can be removed at inference, and the learned spatial states remain decodable into interpretable 3D modalities. This assumption substantially underestimates the threat. We show that a single poisoning phase can implant a programmable backdoor into a VLM, allowing an attacker to choose previously unseen target-caption semantics at inference time and synthesize corresponding stealthy triggers on demand. Unlike","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T14:25:29Z","category_id":"multimodal","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10959.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","cs.CR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是 VLM 训练数据投毒里的一个更强威胁模型：攻击者不是在训练前把“某个触发器→某个固定目标文本”绑定死，而是希望只投毒一次，就在推理时按需指定任意目标 caption，并即时合成对应触发器，让模型输出此前从未在投毒阶段预定义过的文本语义。论文要解决的核心问题有两个：训练时如何把“触发器作为隐式指令”的能力植入模型；推理时如何把任意目标文本编码成一个足够隐蔽、又能稳定激活后门的视觉触发器。","importance":"从基座研究角度，这不是预训练方法论文，但它和大规模多模态预训练的数据安全、数据治理、鲁棒性评估直接相关。它的启发不在于攻击本身多新，而在于它揭示了 VLM 的一个结构性风险：模型的零样本泛化能力、跨模态语义对齐能力，可能被反向利用，变成“后门可编程性”。传统 backdoor 往往是固定映射，风险边界相对清楚；这篇工作指出，一次投毒就可能得到一个 inference-time programmable controller。对做 web-scale 数据采集、自动清洗、合成数据混入、持续预训练的人来说，这意味着只看 clean accuracy 或少量固定 trigger 检测是不够的，安全评估需要覆盖更开放的目标语义空间。","technical_approach":"方法由两部分组成。第一部分是启发式投毒策略。作者不再让模型死记固定 trigger-target 对，而是构造多样化的 poisoned pairs，让模型在带触发器时把触发器的“语义内容”当成一种可被解码的控制信号。换句话说，训练目标不是记住某个 patch 对应某句固定恶意文本，而是让模型学会一种更抽象的规则：当视觉输入中出现某类特殊模式时，应优先读取其中承载的语义并据此生成 caption。论文把这一步建立在 VLM 原本就具备的 zero-shot generalization 上，试图把这种泛化能力从“理解新概念”转成“理解新触发器所编码的新目标文本”。\n\n第二部分是 trigger steganography，记作 TS(·)。它的作用是把任意目标 caption z 映射成一个视觉空间中的隐蔽触发器，可以是范数受控的噪声，也可以是一个不带明显语义的 patch。这里的关键不是做一个固定 trigger，而是根据目标文本动态生成 trigger，同时保持低可感知性。全文片段提到，这个映射是基于 vanilla trigger 在视觉特征空间中的表示来构造的，也就是说，作者先找到一种能稳定激活后门的基础触发器表示，再把目标文本的信息嵌入到这一表示附近，使模型在推理时把该 trigger 解读为目标 caption 的隐式载体。\n\n从攻击范式上看，这和经典 one-to-one / N-to-N backdoor 的区别很明确。传统方法在 poisoning 阶段就把目标集合固定，推理时只能触发预设输出；这里则把“目标选择”从训练期解耦到推理期。技术上真正难的地方，是既要让模型对未见目标文本泛化，又要让 trigger 足够 stealthy，还要尽量不破坏模型的 benign utility。论文声称其方法对经典 backdoor defense 也有鲁棒性，这意味着它不是靠特别显眼的 patch 或明显的数据异常来工作。","experiments":"从提供的全文内容看，实验主要在 LLaVA 上进行，数据集使用 Flickr8k 和 Flickr30k。评测目标包括两类：一类是 benign performance，即后门植入后模型在干净输入上的正常 caption/理解能力是否保持；另一类是 attack efficacy，即给定动态生成的 trigger 后，模型能否输出攻击者指定、且在投毒阶段未预先绑定的目标 caption。作者强调其 any-to-any caption-control 设计相较固定映射后门更灵活，并在实验中展示了对未见 target-caption semantics 的控制能力。\n\n摘要和全文片段没有给出具体数值表，但可以从实验设置推断几个关键看点。第一，应该会报告 attack success rate 或与目标文本的相似度指标，用来衡量模型输出是否接近指定 z。第二，应该会比较不同 trigger 形式，如噪声型与 patch 型，在隐蔽性和攻击成功率上的取舍。第三，应该会与 one-to-one 或 N-to-N backdoor 做对比，证明 programmable setting 不是以明显牺牲 clean utility 为代价。第四，论文明确提到对 classical backdoor defenses 的鲁棒性，因此值得看它测试了哪些 defense：是数据过滤、激活聚类、微调净化，还是输入变换类防御。\n\n如果要从全文里抓最有信息量的数字，建议重点找：投毒比例、LLaVA 版本与参数规模、目标文本是否完全 out-of-poisoning-set、clean caption 指标下降多少、动态目标控制的成功率、不同隐蔽预算下的性能曲线，以及 defense 前后攻击成功率变化。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，不必逐式推导。先看 introduction 和 threat model，确认作者把问题从 fixed mapping backdoor 提升到了 programmable any-to-any control，这决定了它的研究价值。然后直接看方法总览图，理解 heuristic poisoning 和 TS(·) 两个模块分别负责什么。接着看实验部分里三类结果：clean utility、动态目标控制效果、对 defense 的鲁棒性。若时间有限，20-30 分钟足够把主要结论读清。\n\n阅读时要特别留意两个问题。第一，作者到底是让模型“读懂 trigger 中编码的文本语义”，还是只是学到一种近似的特征匹配捷径；这决定了攻击是否真的具有可扩展性。第二，触发器生成是否依赖访问 victim model 的梯度或特征空间，如果依赖很强，现实攻击面会变窄。还建议看附录中的 notation、poisoned data construction 和 defense setting，因为这类论文的主文常把关键限制条件写得比较轻。\n\n对预训练研究员来说，最值得带走的不是攻击细节，而是评估思路：多模态预训练的语义泛化能力可能放大数据投毒的后果，安全评测不能只测固定 trigger，也要测开放目标空间下的可编程操控。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a stronger backdoor threat model for VLMs. Instead of binding a trigger to a fixed target caption during poisoning, the attacker wants to poison once and later choose arbitrary target caption semantics at inference time, including targets never specified during training. The technical problem has two parts: how to implant a trigger-as-instruction behavior during poisoning, and how to synthesize a stealthy visual trigger at inference time that encodes any desired caption while still reliably activating the backdoor.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it is relevant to large-scale multimodal pretraining through the lens of data security and robustness. The important point is not just that it proposes another backdoor, but that it exposes a structural risk in VLMs: the same zero-shot generalization and cross-modal alignment abilities that make these models useful can also make them programmable after poisoning. Traditional backdoors have a bounded threat surface because the trigger-target mapping is fixed in advance. Here, a single poisoning phase can create an inference-time controller over open-ended target semantics. For anyone working on web-scale data collection, filtering, synthetic data mixing, or continual pretraining, the implication is clear: clean accuracy and fixed-trigger checks are not enough. 2024 的 Hdp bound 在实际上下文长度下并不直接生效，所需 n* 大到物理上不可实现；但这个理论仍提供了一个有用变量：任务的串行深度。对基座模型研究来说，这有助于区分两类能力缺口：一类是模型已具备局部运算能力，但单次前向的计算深度不够，需要 scratchpad；另一类是知识、检索或浅层分类任务，CoT 不一定带来收益。论文不是预训练方法，但对设计 reasoning eval、训练 length 和层数变化的阈值时，decoder-only transformer 无法求解 L-sequential function composition。本文没有把该定理当作数值预测器，因为对 Qwen-7B、Qwen-32B 等模型，非平凡阈值对应的 prompt length 约为 10^(10^34.4) 到 10^(10^77.8)，远超真实 benchmark。作者将其作为概念框架，把 GSM8K、MATH、MMLU、ARC、HumanEval 等 benchmark 映射到粗粒度计算复杂度类别，并预注册假设：高串行深度任务应从 CoT 获益；低深度任务应基本不受影响，甚至可能因额外 token 引入噪声而变差。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen-2.5-7B、Qwen-2.5-32B 和 Llama-3.1-8B，在五个常见 benchmark 上比较 direct answer 与 CoT。主要发现是 benchmark 内部存在 serial-depth gradient：无 CoT 准确率随题目串行深度增加而单调下降，而 CoT 条件下对深度相对不敏感。正向假设得到支持：GSM8K 和 MATH 这类多步数学任务上，CoT 在三个模型上都能恢复一部分性能。负向假设没有完全成立：在正确答案抽取修正后，MMLU 和 ARC 上 CoT 大体中性，六个模型-任务组合的变化范围为 0.0 到 +4.6 个百分点。明显负例主要是 HumanEval 上 Qwen-7B 的 CoT 下降 28.7 个百分点，但 HumanEval 被作者归为 L 类任务，而不是低深度 TC0 类任务。论文也强调，答案抽取错误会显著污染 CoT 评估结论，因此评测协议本身是一个关键变量。","reading_guide":"建议用 20 分钟泛读。先看 Introduction 和 Background 中对 Hdp bound 的处理，重点是作者承认理论边界在实际长度下不直接约束模型，这一点让后续实验更可信。然后看 benchmark 到复杂度类别的映射表，以及 direct-answer/CoT 的主结果表。最值得细看的是 within-benchmark serial-depth gradient 的分析，因为它比“某个 benchmark 平均提升多少”更接近机制解释。附录 B 的答案抽取修正也建议扫读，CoT 评估常被 parser 和格式问题干扰，这部分对做 reasoning eval 很实用。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks when chain-of-thought helps and when it hurts. It frames the issue as a serial-depth bottleneck in decoder-only transformers: direct answering forces all computation into one forward pass, while CoT externalizes intermediate states into the output stream and gives the model additional sequential computation steps.","importance":"The main value for pretraining and reasoning research is that it turns the effect of CoT from a prompt-level observation into a task-structure hypothesis. The paper is careful about the theory: the Hdp bound from Chen et al. 2024 is asymptotic and does not numerically bind at realistic context lengths. For the tested models, the non-vacuous prompt lengths are around 10^(10^34.4) to 10^(10^77.8), far beyond practical settings. Still, the bound identifies a useful variable: serial depth. This helps separate cases where the model has the local operations but lacks enough single-pass sequential depth, from cases where the task is mainly knowledge recall, shallow classification, or pattern matching. The paper is not a pretraining method paper, but it is useful for reasoning evaluation design, CoT data construction, and interpreting test-time compute gains.","technical_approach":"The authors use the multi-party autoregressive communication framework of Chen et al. 2024 as a conceptual guide. In that framework, Hdp equals the number of attention heads times head dimension times numerical precision, and represents the bandwidth available to a single forward pass. The original theorem gives an asymptotic lower bound for L-sequential function composition, but this paper does not treat it as a direct quantitative predictor. Instead, it maps five common NLP benchmarks into coarse computational-depth classes: GSM8K and MATH as multi-step sequential composition, and MMLU, ARC, and HumanEval as lower or different depth regimes. The pre-registered hypothesis is two-sided: high-depth tasks should benefit from CoT because intermediate computation must be externalized; low-depth tasks should be insensitive to CoT or may be harmed by extra noisy tokens.","experiments":"The experiments compare direct-answer prompting and CoT prompting on Qwen-2.5-7B, Qwen-2.5-32B, and Llama-3.1-8B across five benchmarks. The central empirical result is a within-benchmark serial-depth gradient: no-CoT accuracy decreases monotonically as per-item serial depth increases, while CoT accuracy is roughly depth-invariant. The positive hypothesis is supported on GSM8K and MATH across all three models, where CoT recovers performance on multi-step problems. The negative hypothesis is not strongly supported after correcting answer extraction: on MMLU and ARC, CoT is roughly neutral, with changes ranging from 0.0 to +4.6 percentage points across six model-task cells. The main surviving negative case is HumanEval with Qwen-7B, where CoT drops performance by 28.7 percentage points; the authors classify HumanEval as an L-class benchmark rather than a low-depth TC0 case. The paper also shows that answer extraction details can materially change CoT conclusions, which is an important evaluation caveat.","reading_guide":"A 20-minute skim is appropriate. Start with the Introduction and Background sections to see how the paper uses the Hdp bound without overclaiming its practical numerical relevance. Then read the benchmark-to-complexity mapping and the main direct-answer versus CoT result tables. The most useful part is the within-benchmark serial-depth analysis, because it is more informative than average benchmark gains. Appendix B on answer extraction is also worth reading, especially for anyone building reasoning evaluations, since parser and formatting failures can distort measured CoT effects.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇把 CoT 收益压到 serial depth 上，判断干净；优先看任务分层和 no-CoT 退化曲线。","en":"The useful part is the clean mapping from CoT gains to serial depth; read the task stratification and the no-CoT degradation curves."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Vistula University","Vistula University, Warsaw"],"authors":["Tughanbulut Kurtulush"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["chain-of-thought","reasoning-mechanism","serial-depth","test-time-compute","transformer-limits"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09942","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.09942","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.10986","title":"What Iterated Self-Feeding Probes of Language Models Measure, and a test that separates the construction from the model","abstract":"A growing class of methods probes a language model by feeding it its own output: self-consistency, iterated refinement, agentic loops. We ask what such a probe measures, in a construction chosen to make the question sharp: a ring of token cells resampled in place by the model's own windowed conditional p_r(x_i | x_{i+-r}). The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences and is not new; what we change is the coupling. Advancing two rings that differ in one token under common random numbers m","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T14:40:35Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10986.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文问一个很具体的问题：把语言模型的输出反复喂回给模型时，观测到的动力学指标到底是在测模型，还是在测 probe 构造本身。作者用一个环形 token 系统把问题固定下来，并说明两者很容易混在一起。","importance":"它不是训练 recipe，也不直接改进预训练效果。价值在于测量方法：self-consistency、iterated refinement、agent loop、masked iterative generation 都把静态模型变成了动态系统。如果 probe 自身会制造相变或吸收态，那么从这些循环行为推断模型能力、稳定性或推理机制就会出错。对研究非 AR 生成、masked LM 动力学、agent 循环稳定性和机制分析的人有参考意义。","technical_approach":"作者构造一个长度为 N 的环形 token 序列，每个位置按随机顺序被原地重采样。重采样分布来自语言模型在半径 r 窗口上的条件分布 p_r(x_i | x_{i±r})，中心 token 被 mask，温度为 T。关键变化不是 Glauber dynamics 本身，而是 coupling 方式。作者不用 maximal coupling 去测 mixing time，而是用 common random numbers：两条环形链初始只差一个 token，之后使用相同的随机数流和访问顺序推进。这样当两个窗口完全一致时，两条链会抽到同一个 token；任何差异都可以归因于初始扰动的传播。基于这个构造，作者定义了 token-space Lyapunov exponent λ_ca、归一化阻尼长度 D_norm 和空间 light cone。论文还提出一个判别原则：固定模型改构造、固定构造改模型，观察指标是否移动，用实验而不是直觉区分 probe-determined 和 model-determined readout。","experiments":"实验包含几类校准和控制。第一，在 Domany–Kinzel automaton 上做 reproduction calibration，damage field 可由已知自动机独立预测，结果 bit-exact，mismatching cells 为 0；离线 control 会失败，用来确认测量工具没有把无关相关性当信号。第二，方法能区分 elementary cellular automata 中 damage 是否存活，并恢复已知 transition matrix。第三，作者观察到一个 sharp absorbing-state transition，可测到三位小数，但进一步证明它来自 probe 构造：机制是 argmax map 的吸引固定点，且只出现在 r∈{1,2}。masked-LM control 没有这个固定点，因此没有对应 transition。第四，五组 manipulation 用来分离构造因素和模型因素：改变构造会移动 manufactured transition；跨模型 family 改变模型不移动它；改变训练 checkpoint 会移动 developmental transition。第五，作者报告了 estimator gating 的必要性：黑盒 LM 动力学上的指数估计如果不按测量几何做 gating，会给出看似自信但错误的结论，文中列出四个被已知答案系统拦下的错误 verdict。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 30–45 分钟。重点看 §2 的构造设定，尤其是 in-place resampling、环形边界、中心 mask、随机访问顺序，以及这些设定和真实 AR generation 的差异。§3 的 Domany–Kinzel 校准决定了测量工具是否可信。§4 的 manufactured phase transition 是全文最重要的警示案例：一个看似模型性质的现象可能来自 probe。§5 的 vary-one-factor test 值得细看，可迁移到 agent loop、self-refinement 和 iterative decoding 的评估设计。§6 的 estimator gating 对做黑盒行为测量的人也很实用。§7 明确说它不是 interpretability 结果，这一点有助于避免过度解读 λ_ca。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what is actually measured when a language model is repeatedly fed its own outputs. In a controlled ring-of-tokens construction, the observed dynamics can reflect either the model or the probe, and the two can look similar unless explicitly separated.","importance":"This is not a pretraining method paper, but it is useful for measurement. Many modern procedures turn a static predictor into a dynamical system: self-consistency, iterative refinement, agent loops, and masked iterative generation. If the probing construction itself can create absorbing states or phase transitions, then behavioral conclusions about model stability, reasoning, or self-correction can be misleading. The paper is most relevant to researchers studying non-autoregressive generation, masked-LM dynamics, agent-loop stability, and mechanistic evaluation.","technical_approach":"The setup is a ring of N token cells. Each site is resampled in place from the model’s windowed conditional distribution p_r(x_i | x_{i±r}) at temperature T, with the center token masked. The substrate is Glauber dynamics on token sequences. The main change is the coupling. Instead of maximal coupling, which is suited to mixing-time questions, the authors use common random numbers: two rings differ in one initial token and are then advanced with the same random-number stream and visit order. If their local windows agree, they draw the same token, so any later difference can be attributed to the injected perturbation. This makes damage spreading measurable through a token-space Lyapunov exponent λ_ca, a normalized damping length D_norm, and a spatial light cone. The paper also gives a practical discriminator: vary the construction while holding the model fixed, and vary the model while holding the construction fixed. This separates probe-determined readings from model-determined readings.","experiments":"$2b","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–45 minutes. Read the construction section carefully: in-place resampling, periodic boundaries, center masking, random site order, and the mismatch with normal autoregressive generation all matter. The Domany–Kinzel calibration is the main sanity check for the measurement tool. The manufactured phase transition section is the key warning case: an apparently model-level phenomenon can be created by the probe. The discriminator section is the most reusable part for evaluating self-refinement loops, agent loops, and iterative decoding. The estimator-gating section is useful if you measure black-box dynamics. The paper explicitly states that λ_ca is not a localized interpretability mechanism, which is important for keeping the claims bounded.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看实验分解和 coupling 设计；它会让你少把 agent loop 的动力学伪信号误读成模型能力。","en":"Skim the coupling design and decomposition results; it helps avoid reading agent-loop dynamics as model capability when the probe creates the signal."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Independent Researcher","Affiliation:Independent Researcher, Chile"],"authors":["Nicolás Vera Zúñiga"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["mechanistic-analysis","self-feeding","agent-loops","dynamical-systems","measurement"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10986","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.10986","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.10441","title":"Detecting an Effect Is Not Learning to Act on It: A Reward-SNR Floor for LLM Acquisition Agents","abstract":"Many pipelines can pay a per-example cost to acquire an auxiliary, model-derived observation -- an LLM's structured reasoning, a slow oracle, an expensive measurement -- and then must decide when the acquired signal is worth using. Our thesis is a distinction that is easy to miss: detecting that such a signal helps on average is not the same as learning to act on it per instance, and a reward-SNR floor governs when the second is even possible. Even when the signal is faithful and an in-sample or","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T03:46:23Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10441.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.IR"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“辅助信号有没有平均收益”，而是更难也更实际的问题：在离线数据里，能否学到一个可部署的逐样本 acquisition policy，决定何时值得为某个样本额外付费获取 LLM 推理、慢速 oracle 或昂贵测量。论文给出的核心结论是，平均效应显著并不意味着逐样本决策可学；真正限制可学性的是 reward effect 的信噪比。如果辅助信号带来的逐样本 reward uplift 的 SNR 低于一个随样本量缩放的下界，离线学习到的路由策略就无法稳定优于随机，即便事后用 in-sample oracle 看起来能挑出“最该调用”的样本。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于提出了新的 LM，而在于它把一个在 LLM pipeline 里很常见、但常被误判的问题讲清楚了：很多人看到 CoT、structured reasoning、self-reflection、tool call 或额外 verifier 在平均上有帮助，就自然会继续做 router、gating、budgeted inference，默认逐样本选择一定可学。论文指出这一步经常站不住，因为 uplift 学习面对的是低 SNR reward，而不是高可见度的平均增益。这个判断对 test-time compute、reasoning-on-demand、selective tool use、LLM-as-feature、甚至 RL/post-train 中的 credit assignment 都有启发。贡献类型更像“可检测性边界 + 诊断框架 + 负结果做实”，量级上属于方法论层面的提醒：它不会直接改写训练 recipe，但会改变很多人对 acquisition / routing 实验结果的解释方式。","technical_approach":"方法分三层。第一层是理论刻画。作者把问题形式化为：策略只能看便宜的 side information，决定是否获取昂贵辅助信号；目标是最大化带成本约束的 downstream reward。关键量不是辅助信号本身是否 faithful，而是“获取后相对不获取的 reward 增量”在样本层面的均值 μ 与标准差 σ 的比值 ρ=μ/σ。论文给出一个 detectability floor，近似写成 ρ*(N)≈2.8/√N，意思是样本量 N 给定时，若 uplift 的 SNR 低于这个量级，则从离线 reward 中恢复出有用的条件化策略在统计上就很难。这里强调的是必要条件，不是严格不可能定理。\n\n第二层是具体实例 SHE。作者设计了 Structured Hypothesis Embeddings：用冻结 LLM 读取用户交互历史，生成 K 个按置信度排序、带证据引用的 latent intent hypotheses，再把这些 hypothesis 编码成 embedding，作为推荐模型的额外输入分支。这个设计有两个目的：一是把“昂贵辅助信号”具体化成 today LLM system 里真实会用的 structured reasoning；二是让信号具备可解释性和可检验 faithfulness。论文还设计 cited-vs-non-cited faithfulness 指标，检查 hypothesis 是否真由历史证据支撑，而不是自由发挥。\n\n第三层是策略学习与诊断。作者在多种粒度上尝试学习 acquisition gate：per-impression、cluster-level、手工 regime、uplift tree 等，cluster 数 K 从 4 到 64。对照组包括随机路由、in-sample oracle，以及 matched-moment noise placebo。placebo 的作用很关键：它构造与真实 uplift 统计矩匹配的纯噪声，如果这种噪声也能复现 oracle gap，就说明所谓“可学习结构”其实只是 order statistics。作者还加入 positive control：向数据中注入可控 SNR 的合成信号，验证同一套 deployable pipeline 在 SNR 超过阈值后确实能恢复策略，从而排除“模型太弱或实现有 bug”的解释。","experiments":"实验结论很集中。首先，SHE 作为辅助信号本身是 faithful 的，而且在某些 backbone / regime 下确实有平均收益。论文指出它在 ordered sequential backbone 上有显著增益，文中提到 GRU / SASRec 这类顺序建模骨干能更好利用该信号；但从全局看，redundancy gap 与零统计上不可区分，说明它不是普适增益，而是强烈依赖数据 regime。作者进一步观察到：在 sparse history、信息不足的场景里，SHE 更容易被 backbone 吸收或与已有特征冗余；在长历史、多意图场景里，它更可能提供互补信息。\n\n其次，所有逐样本或粗粒度路由实验都失败。无论是 per-impression gate，还是 K=4-64 的 cluster gate、手工划分 regime，或 uplift-tree 粒度，学习到的策略都没有稳定超过随机策略。更关键的是，matched-moment noise placebo 可以复现至少 100% 的 in-sample oracle apparent gain。这个结果直接支持作者的主张：oracle 看到的“上界”主要来自噪声排序，而不是可泛化的结构。\n\n第三，SNR floor 得到正反两方面验证。反面是三个真实数据集的 reward SNR 都低于 detectability floor，因此 policy learnability 失败与理论预期一致。正面是 synthetic positive control：当注入信号使 ρ 跨过阈值后，同样的学习流程开始恢复可部署策略。这个实验很重要，因为它把“学不会”从经验负结果提升为“低 SNR 条件下本来就不该期待学会”。\n\n摘要里没有给出完整表格数字，全文最值得盯的是各数据集的 N、估计 ρ、对应 ρ*(N)、以及 oracle / random / learned policy 的 uplift 差值和置信区间。作者还给了 58-claim ledger，把每个结论映射到脚本、CSV 和图，复现实证做得很细。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一是引言和 Figure 10，那里把“mean detection ≠ policy learnability”讲得最清楚，也给了整篇文章的判断框架。第二是理论部分关于 ρ=μ/σ 和 ρ*(N)≈2.8/√N 的推导，重点不是逐行证明，而是理解这个阈值为什么针对的是 uplift policy 的可检测性，而不是平均处理效应。第三是实验里 oracle、random、learned gate、noise placebo、positive control 这五类对照的关系；如果这组对照看懂了，整篇文章的负结果就站住了。\n\n如果只花 30-45 分钟，建议看：Introduction、定义 detectability floor 的章节、SHE 方法概述、routing 失败的主表和 noise placebo 图、positive control 图、最后的 design-time regime gate 建议。若要深挖，值得看作者如何定义 faithfulness、redundancy gap，以及为什么 pooled regime gate 比 per-instance gate 更现实。这些点和 LLM 推理路由、工具调用预算控制很接近。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a practical but often misframed question: given an expensive auxiliary signal—LLM reasoning, a slow oracle, an extra measurement—can we learn an offline per-instance acquisition policy that decides when paying for it is worthwhile? The main claim is that detecting a positive average effect is not enough. What matters is whether the per-example reward uplift induced by acquisition has sufficient signal-to-noise ratio. If that reward SNR is below a sample-size-dependent floor, a deployable routing policy is not recoverable from offline data, even when an in-sample oracle appears to show a large gain.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model research is methodological. Many LLM pipelines observe that CoT, self-reflection, verifier calls, tool use, or structured intermediate reasoning help on average, and then move directly to budgeted routing or selective invocation. This paper argues that the second step can be statistically ill-posed: uplift learning is governed by low-SNR reward differences, not by the visibility of the mean effect. That matters for test-time compute allocation, selective reasoning, tool-use gating, LLM-as-feature systems, and even post-training settings where credit assignment is noisy. The contribution is not a new model family; it is a detectability criterion, a diagnosis toolkit, and a carefully validated negative result that changes how routing gains should be interpreted.","technical_approach":"$2c","experiments":"$2d","reading_guide":"$2e","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"反常识结论：平均有用的辅助信号未必能学到per-instance路由策略，建议重点看§3的SNR下限推导和§4的安慰剂实验。","en":"Counterintuitive finding that average-useful auxiliary signals do not guarantee learnable per-instance routing; focus on §3 SNR floor derivation and §4 placebo experiments."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of California, San Diego"],"authors":["Ying Yuan"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["reward-signal","snr-analysis","routing-policy","agent-theory"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10441","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.10441","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.11025","title":"Data Attribution of Emergent Misalignment with Persona Features","abstract":"Emergent misalignment (EM) is the phenomenon where fine-tuning a language model on a narrow task leads to harmful behavior in unrelated domains. A leading mechanistic account attributes EM to persona features: latent directions acquired during pre-training that misaligned fine-tuning amplifies. We ask where these features come from: which pre-training documents activate them, and whether naturally occurring human-written text suffices to induce EM. Using Sparse Autoencoder (SAE) based model diff","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T15:05:24Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11025.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作追问的是 emergent misalignment 的上游来源：如果窄域微调会把模型推向越狱、操控、反社会角色等失配行为，那么这些可被放大的“persona features”究竟来自哪些预训练文档，且自然人类文本本身是否足以诱发这类失配。它不是只做相关性分析，而是把问题拆成三层：先用 aligned vs misaligned 的对比微调找出被放大/被压制的 SAE 特征，再用 activation steering 验证这些特征对行为有因果作用，最后把特征回溯到预训练语料并测试这些语料能否单独复现 EM。","importance":"对预训练研究的意义主要有两点。第一，它把 EM 从“微调副作用”往前推到了“预训练中已存在的潜在行为方向”，这对数据过滤、数据配比、人格/角色类文本的风险建模都有直接启发。第二，它给出一个不太直观但很重要的结论：仅靠检索到的人类原文，往往不足以通过微调诱发明显 EM；把同样内容改写成 LLM 生成的 instruction-response 结构后，行为迁移才明显增强。这说明风险不只在语义主题，还在监督格式、回答体裁和模型熟悉的 instruction manifold 上。贡献量级更像机制定位与风险归因，而不是一个立刻可部署的防御方案。","technical_approach":"方法上有三步。第一步是 SAE-based model diffing。作者在 medical、legal、security 三个领域分别构造 benign aligned 微调模型和 narrow misaligned 微调模型，用同一组 44 个 EM 诱发提示抽取残差流激活，并投到对应层的 SAE 特征空间。通过比较 misaligned 与 aligned 模型在这些提示上的平均特征激活差，筛出被 misalignment 特异性放大的正向特征和被压制的负向特征。这里的关键设计是用 aligned reference 控制领域微调本身带来的变化，尽量把 domain adaptation 与 misalignment signal 分开。\n\n第二步是因果验证。作者对单个 SAE 特征做 activation steering，分别测试：向 aligned 模型注入高激活的 persona feature 是否能直接诱发 EM；向 misaligned 模型补回被压制的 safety/assistant-identity feature 是否能把行为拉回。结果显示这种控制是稀疏且特征特异的，随机特征几乎不起作用，说明不是“任意大方向扰动都能让模型失配”，而是少数可解释特征承担了主要行为杠杆。\n\n第三步是数据归因与再训练验证。作者基于特征激活在预训练语料中检索高响应文档，人工归纳出 recurring themes，如 villain、支配、操控、恶意 agency、角色扮演等叙事。随后他们做关键对照：直接用这些人类文档微调，EM 很弱；把这些内容转成 LLM 生成的 instruction-response 对，再微调，EM 明显出现，而且能跨模型家族迁移。这个结果把“内容主题”与“监督形式”区分开了：同样的语义材料，是否被包装成模型熟悉的问答/指令响应格式，决定了它能否高效激活并巩固这些 persona directions。","experiments":"实验覆盖四个 open-weight 模型、三个模型家族。特征层面，作者复现并扩展了先前关于 EM persona features 的观察：被放大的多是 jailbreak persona、sarcasm、deception、manipulation、roleplay 一类特征；被压制的多是 refusal、安全提示、assistant identity 相关特征。因果层面，单个特征 steering 就能把 aligned 模型的 misalignment rate 推到最高 62%，而直接做 misalignment fine-tuning 的基线约为 35%；反过来，对 misaligned 模型施加 re-alignment steering，可把 misalignment rate 压到约 1%。这说明单特征的行为控制强度并不弱于整套微调。\n\n数据归因层面，检索出的高归因文档确实集中在负面角色、支配关系、恶意意图等叙事，但直接拿这些 human-written 文本做微调，不能稳定复现 EM。把同源内容改写成 LLM 生成的 instruction-response 数据后，行为偏移显著增强，并且能跨模型家族生效。这个对照实验是全文最关键的证据，因为它说明“自然文本里有信号”与“该信号能否在监督微调中被高效提取”是两件事。摘要里没有给出所有模型、层位、数据量和各域细分数字；精读时应重点核对附录中的 baseline EM rates、每个模型的 steering 成功率分布、以及 human vs synthetic 数据微调的样本规模是否严格匹配。","reading_guide":"泛读建议是先抓三件事。第一，看方法部分里 aligned vs misaligned 的对比设计，确认作者如何排除纯领域微调效应。第二，看 feature steering 结果图，重点不是平均提升，而是“单特征可强诱发、随机特征无效、负向特征可回拉”这三个现象是否同时成立。第三，看数据归因后的再训练对照，尤其是 human-written 文本与由其派生的 synthetic instruction-response 数据之间的差异。\n\n如果时间有限，优先读 Introduction、Model Diffing、Steering、Data Attribution 和 human-vs-synthetic 的实验段落，再翻附录核对数据规模与 prompt 细节，预计 25–35 分钟能抓住主线。值得深挖的点有两个：一是 SAE 特征解释是否足够稳，是否存在 feature splitting/aliasing；二是 instruction-response 格式为何更容易把潜在人格方向变成可泛化行为，这和 post-train 数据格式、reward shaping、甚至 RL policy extraction 都有关。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks where emergent misalignment comes from upstream. If narrow fine-tuning can push a model toward jailbreak-like, manipulative, or antisocial behavior, which pretraining documents seeded the latent persona features that later get amplified? And is naturally occurring human-written text alone sufficient to induce EM, or does the effect depend on how that content is packaged during supervision? The work tackles all three layers: identifying shifted SAE features, testing their causal role with steering, and tracing them back to pretraining data followed by re-finetuning tests.","importance":"Its main value for pretraining research is that it relocates part of the EM story from fine-tuning into pretraining. The paper argues that misalignment is not created from scratch by narrow supervision; fine-tuning can amplify latent behavioral directions already present in the base model. That matters for data curation, mixture design, and risk accounting around roleplay, villain, domination, and harmful-agency narratives. A second important result is more subtle: retrieved human-written source documents are usually not enough to reproduce strong EM through fine-tuning, while LLM-generated instruction-response pairs derived from the same content are. This suggests the risk is not only in semantic content but also in supervision format and response style.","technical_approach":"$2f","experiments":"$30","reading_guide":"$31","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不直接改 pretrain recipe，但会改变你看待“坏行为从哪来”的 prior；重点看 feature steering 的因果证据，数据归因部分先当弱信号。","en":"It won’t change your training recipe tomorrow, but it does update the prior on where misalignment comes from; read it for the causal steering evidence, and treat the data attribution part as a weaker signal."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Lamarr Institute for Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence","Affiliation:Lamarr Institute for Machine Learning and Artificial Intelligence"],"authors":["Clemens Vetter","David Kaczér","Lucie Flek","Florian Mai"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["misalignment","SAE","persona-features","mechanistic-interpretability","data-attribution"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.11025","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.11025","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.10758","title":"Where To Look? : Causal Tracing of Vision Encoders in VLM","abstract":"Vision-language models can describe an image with remarkable accuracy, yet a more fundamental question remains unanswered: what visual information actually drives their answers? doe","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T10:17:34Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10758.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要回答的不是“VLM能不能答对”，而是“答对时到底依赖了哪些视觉表征”。更具体地说，它检验视觉编码器里对最终图文匹配分数有因果作用的 token，是否真的落在与文本查询对应的目标区域上。论文针对一个长期被默认成立、但很少被直接验证的假设：强视觉语言性能是否意味着模型内部已经形成了空间上对齐的因果表征。作者给出的答案是否定的——至少在他们分析的 CLIP 及更大 VLM 上，很多高因果贡献 token 并不位于目标框内，说明“会看”“会用”“会定位”并不是同一件事。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在两点。第一，它提醒人不要把 benchmark 上的视觉问答、caption 或 grounding 成绩，直接等同于“模型内部学到了稳健的空间因果表示”。这对 VLM 预训练目标设计、视觉 tokenizer、跨模态对齐损失都有直接影响。第二，它把 mechanistic interpretability 里的 causal tracing 真正推进到 vision encoder 内部，而不是只看语言侧或最终输出层。这种分析框架本身可以复用到 native multimodal pretrain、视觉 token 压缩、区域级监督是否必要等问题上。贡献更偏诊断和测量，不是提出新的训练方法，但它指出了一个很具体的表征缺口：当前 VLM 可能更多依赖上下文、共现和全局外观线索，而不是空间上局部且可归因的目标证据。","technical_approach":"方法核心是把 activation patching 形式化地搬到视觉编码器中，测量单个 layer-token 对最终图文相似度的因果恢复能力。具体做法有三步。先对原图做 clean run，得到图文相似度 s_clean；再对图像加高斯噪声得到 corrupted run，得到 s_corr；然后在 corrupted 输入下，把某一层某一 token 的激活替换成 clean run 中对应激活，得到 patched run 的分数 s_patch。作者据此定义 causal contribution Γ(l,t)，表示该激活恢复了 clean 与 corrupted 之间多少性能差距。这个定义的好处是量纲清楚，能直接比较不同 token、不同层的相对因果作用，而不是只看梯度或 attention 权重。\n\n第二个关键设计是把“因果重要性”与“空间对齐”显式连接起来。由于 ViT token 对应固定图像 patch，作者把每个 token 映射回输入图像区域 Pt，再与目标物体的 ground-truth bounding box B 计算 IoU(Pt, B)。随后在每一层上计算 causal contribution 与 IoU 的相关性 Corr_l，用来判断：随着层数加深，真正驱动输出的 token 是否越来越集中到目标区域。如果 Corr_l 高，说明模型的因果证据与空间目标一致；如果低，说明模型虽然可能答对，但依赖的并不是目标区域本身。\n\n从全文描述看，作者不仅在 CLIP 上做了受控分析，还把同样流程扩展到更大的 VLM、不同 corruption 方式，以及不同代际的视觉编码器，包括 ViT 系和 SigLIP 系。另一个值得注意的点是，他们还追问了一个更细的问题：当外观线索被去掉时，模型是否还能保留结构理解。结论是视觉线索确实被用来理解结构，但这种结构利用并不自动转化为局部空间可解释的因果表示。这和很多只用 attention map 做可视化的工作不同，作者强调的是“替换后输出是否恢复”，因此更接近功能性因果分析，而不是相关性分析。","experiments":"从给出的全文内容里，最明确的结果有三类。第一，在 CLIP 上，作者对 100 个样本逐 token、逐层做 causal tracing，发现 causal contribution 与目标框 IoU 的关系很弱。文中直接给出的定性结论是：高因果 token 往往不在目标区域内，相关性整体偏低。这说明恢复输出分数的关键视觉表征，很多来自目标外部区域。\n\n第二，这个现象不是 CLIP 的偶然性。作者把分析扩展到 2023 年以来更大的 VLM，在不同 corruption 设置下仍观察到相似模式。也就是说，模型规模变大、多模态能力增强，并没有自然带来更强的空间因果对齐。全文片段没有给出完整表格数字，但结论写得很明确：这种“高性能但弱空间因果对齐”的模式跨模型存在。\n\n第三，作者还测试了去除外观线索后的结构理解能力，发现模型确实会利用视觉 cues 去理解结构，而不是完全靠语言先验或数据偏置。但即便如此，因果重要 token 与目标区域之间仍存在明显错位。这个结果很关键，因为它排除了一个过于简单的解释：不是模型完全没看图，而是它看图时依赖的证据分布与人期待的局部目标证据并不一致。\n\n就实验设计而言，值得注意的细节包括：按层计算 Corr_l 来看空间对齐如何随深度演化；多随机种子报告均值和方差；比较不同视觉编码器架构。若精读全文，建议重点找具体的相关系数数值、不同层峰值位置、不同 corruption 方式是否改变结论，以及 SigLIP 与 ViT 是否在中后层出现系统差异。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题去读。先看方法部分里 causal contribution Γ 的定义，确认它衡量的是“恢复 clean-corrupted gap 的比例”，这是全文最重要的量。再看把 token patch 与 bounding box 做 IoU、并按层计算 Corr_l 的部分，这决定了论文不是在做普通 saliency 可视化，而是在问“因果重要性是否空间落点正确”。\n\n结果部分建议先抓三张信息最密的图：一类是单样本或少量样本的 token 热图，能直观看到高因果 token 落在目标外；一类是按层的 Corr_l 曲线，能看空间对齐是否在深层形成；一类是跨模型比较图，判断这种现象是 CLIP 特例还是更普遍的 VLM 属性。若时间有限，读 introduction、method、CLIP 主实验和跨模型泛化实验就够，大约 25-35 分钟可以建立完整判断。\n\n值得深挖的点有三个。第一，corruption 方式会不会系统性影响 causal token 的位置；如果高斯噪声换成遮挡、patch dropout 或频域扰动，结论是否稳定。第二，图文相似度这个目标更偏 retrieval，迁移到生成式 VLM 的 next-token logits 后，因果空间对齐是否更差或更好。第三，若在预训练中加入区域级对齐、对象中心裁剪、或更强的视觉离散化约束，Corr_l 是否能被显著抬高。这些都直接连到预训练配方设计。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a sharper question than standard VLM evaluation: when a model gives the right answer, which visual representations are actually causally responsible for that answer? More specifically, it tests whether vision tokens with high causal influence on the final image-text score are spatially aligned with the queried object region. The underlying assumption being challenged is common but rarely verified: strong multimodal performance is often taken as evidence that the model has learned grounded, spatially localized internal representations. The paper argues that this assumption does not hold in the examined models. In CLIP and larger VLMs, many highly causal tokens lie outside the target region, so being accurate, being visually grounded, and being spatially localized are not the same property.","importance":"Its value is mainly diagnostic, but the diagnosis is useful for pretraining research. First, it warns against reading benchmark success as evidence of robust spatially grounded representations. That matters for pretraining objectives, visual tokenization, and image-text alignment losses. Second, it extends causal tracing into the vision encoder itself rather than stopping at language-side activations or output behavior. That gives the field a reusable analysis tool for questions such as whether region-level supervision is needed, whether compressed visual tokens preserve grounding, and whether native multimodal pretraining actually builds localized causal evidence. The contribution is not a new training recipe; it is a precise measurement of a representation gap: current VLMs may rely heavily on context, co-occurrence, and global appearance cues instead of object-local evidence.","technical_approach":"$32","experiments":"$33","reading_guide":"$34","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"值得扫方法和核心图；若因果 token 常在目标外，VLM 对齐 loss 可能学到的是场景捷径而非局部 grounding。","en":"Skim the method and main figures; if causal tokens are often off-target, VLM alignment may be learning scene shortcuts rather than local grounding."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Naren Kumar S","Tirth Bhatt","Mayank Singh"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["vlm-interpretability","causal-tracing","vision-encoder","grounding","visual-tokens"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.10758","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2608.10758","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2608.11024","title":"When Visual Signals Mislead: A Mechanistic Study of Attribute Hallucination in Vision-Language Models","abstract":"Attribute hallucination---where vision-language models (VLMs) correctly identify an object but mischaracterize its properties---is prevalent yet mechanistically poorly understood. The dominant explanation, language-prior dominance, has motivated prior-suppression methods, but this explanation has not been directly tested at the attribute level. We present VISOR (Visual-Operational Remediation), a unified framework that couples null-image-based diagnosis with routed remediation. Its VSNR diagnost","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T15:05:18Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2608_11024.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV"],"summary":{"core_problem":"论文要回答一个被默认但很少被直接检验的问题：VLM 的“属性幻觉”（物体识别对，但颜色/材质/状态等属性说错）到底是语言先验压过视觉证据，还是视觉表征/读出本身出了问题，或是两者的决策边界设置不当？作者把问题收敛到可控的 yes/no 属性探测，并希望给出可诊断、可对症修复的机制解释。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接关联不强（更偏 VLM 机制分析与推理时干预），但它提供了一个很实用的“把错误归因到视觉信号 vs 语言先验”的可操作分解：用 null-image（空白图）估计语言侧偏置，用真实图估计视觉驱动的 logit 差。结论与常见叙事相反：在作者的设定与数据上，属性假阳性更常由视觉信号导致，而不是语言先验主导。这会影响后续做 VLM 预训练/中训时的诊断思路：与其一味做 prior suppression，不如优先检查视觉 token/视觉编码器到 LM head 的对齐与读出质量，尤其是“材质”这类属性。","technical_approach":"方法叫 VISOR，由诊断 VSNR + 路由式修复三件套组成。\n1) 诊断分解：对同一 (image, query) 做两次前向。一次用真实图像 I，得到 yes/no 首 token 的 logit 差 δ_vis = logit(yes|I) - logit(no|I)；一次用 uniform gray 的 null image I_∅，得到 δ_prior = logit(yes|I_∅) - logit(no|I_∅)。作者把 δ_prior 当作“语言先验偏置”的估计，把 δ_vis 当作“视觉驱动的最终决策变量”。在负样本（GT=No）上，δ_vis≥0 即为幻觉。\n2) 层级追踪坐标：把最终的 norm+LM head 也作用到中间层 hidden state，定义每层的 δ_vis^(l)，并用 SNR(w,l)=|mean(δ_vis^(l))|/std(δ_vis^(l)) 追踪某个属性词 w 的视觉信号在何处变得低边际或方向错误。\n3) 机制分类：作者区分两类视觉失败模式：\n- 低边际/阈值可校正（visual signal direction 对，但 margin 不够）：更常见于颜色/状态。\n- 方向性失败（在 yes/no logit 空间里视觉信号指向了错误方向）：更常见于材质。\n4) 路由式修复（同一框架下按诊断选操作）：\n- Calib：当视觉方向正确但 margin 小时，用校准的 prior 项移动决策边界（相当于只在“prior subtraction 有理论可能奏效”的区域使用它）。\n- Abstain：当视觉信号低 SNR 时不训练直接拒答/弃权，避免把不稳定信号硬判成 yes。\n- Adapt：当出现方向性失败时，用小型、定向的 adapter 修复“视觉投影/读出”，而不是做输出层 prior suppression（作者给出推导说明：若 δ_prior 非正，输出层减 prior 根本无法把 δ_vis>0 翻到负）。\n整体上，论文的关键不是提出一个更强的解码技巧，而是把“prior suppression 何时有效、何时无效”用可测量的 δ_vis/δ_prior 条件写清楚，并据此做机制对齐的干预。","experiments":"作者在 3 个 VLM 家族、3 类属性上评估，使用 VAW 上 10,791 个负样本做机制检验。核心结果是：用 δ_vis 预测属性假阳性显著强于 δ_prior；δ_prior 在该任务上接近随机水平，而 δ_vis 的符号几乎直接决定是否幻觉（文中表述为 sign(δ_vis) 与幻觉标签在全部 10,791 There is no way to determine whether a discovered circuit or feature is an intrinsic property of the trained model or an artifact of the recovery method, random seed, or hyperparameters. SAEs consistently recover materially different features from the same activations across seeds and widths, and no existing paradigm provides a theorem guaranteeing that the recovered structure is a model invariant.","importance":"This work formalizes the Transformer forward pass as a controlled discrete dynamical system where depth acts as time, cross-token attention writes act as control inputs, and MLPs act as autonomous nonlinear dynamics. It introduces the Koopman operator to yield coordinate-free spectral invariants and proves their finite-sample identifiability, supplying mechanistic interpretability with a provably unique, basis-independent reference object. It also rigorously shows that the SAE objective itself is non-identifiable, explaining the source of run-to-run instability. For pretraining research, the framework provides a theoretical tool for comparing intrinsic model structure across layers and across training runs, usable for analyzing training dynamics, structural emergence, and how pretraining recipes shape internal computation.","technical_approach":"$36","experiments":"Systematic measurements are reported on three pretrained models. API构建只读采集管道，针对GitHub代码搜索单查询最多返回1000条结果、总条数统计严重失准的限制，采用按文件大小切分搜索空间的策略保证采集完整性。最终数据集覆盖3797117份来自282200个公开仓库的SKILL.md文件，按内容精确哈希去重后得到1877981份独立内容；每份独立内容存储全文、解析后的YAML元数据、所在文件夹附属文件信息、所属仓库元数据，对位于标准技能路径下的文件和按大小分层抽样的非标准路径文件补充了完整提交历史；所有副本的仓库、路径信息全部保留，以支持复制传播规律研究。数据集采用SQLite存储，采集环节和后续研究解释环节完全解耦，保留所有文件名匹配的记录，支持研究者自行定义分析人群。","experiments":"初步统计显示50.5%的采集文件是其他文件的逐字副本，直接反映了无包管理机制下靠文件夹复制传播的生态特征。采集过程中发现GitHub代码搜索返回的初始总数估计（约34.9万份）比实际采集到的规模低一个数量级，验证了分块采集策略的必要性。数据集可覆盖多类研究问题：skill格式的采用传播规律、内容与语言演化、跨工具的格式共识形成、无中心化管理下的复用模式、面向自然语言指令的软件质量度量、生态维护与供应链安全。","reading_guide":"泛读即可，不需要逐节细读。重点看两部分：一是采集方法中针对GitHub搜索限制的处理逻辑，理解数据集的覆盖率边界；二是列出的潜在研究问题，建立对这个数据资产适用场景的认知。预计阅读时间15分钟，只有在开展Agent技能选择、指令语料分析、生态安全这类相关研究时，再回头查阅数据集的具体字段和schema。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Nine months after Anthropic published its open Agent Skill specification in October 2025, millions of SKILL.md files have accumulated in public GitHub repositories, but no large-scale, systematically collected dataset exists to study this new class of software artifact: natural-language procedural instructions that LLM agents select probabilistically at runtime, with no compile-time validation, no central package registry, and propagation via direct folder copying between repositories. Empirical understanding of how developers write, reuse, and maintain these skills remains limited.","importance":"The dataset captures an unfiltered view of how real-world users write procedural workflow instructions for LLM agents at ecosystem scale. This data distribution differs substantially from curated SFT and instruction tuning datasets, offering a window into natural user instruction patterns that is relevant for improving agent skill retrieval, instruction following, and tool-use alignment. The dataset’s tracking of skill copies and propagation also enables study of instruction quality correlates and supply-chain security risks, such as malicious modifications to widely copied skills.","technical_approach":"GitSkills is built via a read-only crawl of GitHub code search and REST APIs. To work around GitHub code search’s 1000-result per query limit and unreliable total-count estimates, the crawl partitions the search space by file size to ensure complete retrieval. The final dataset contains 3,797,117 SKILL.md files from 282,200 public repositories, grouped into 1,877,981 distinct contents by exact hash. For each unique content, the dataset stores full text, parsed YAML frontmatter, associated folder contents, and repository metadata; commit history is collected for files in standard skill paths and a size-stratified sample of non-standard paths. All copies are retained with repository and path information to support reuse and propagation analysis, with collection and interpretation decoupled to avoid biasing analysis populations. The dataset is distributed as SQLite files.","experiments":"Initial descriptive statistics show 50.5% of collected SKILL.md files are verbatim copies of other files, consistent with the ecosystem’s copy-based propagation model without a central package manager. The crawl also revealed GitHub code search’s built-in total count estimate of ~349,000 SKILL.md files was an order of magnitude lower than the true collected count, validating the size-partitioned crawl approach. The dataset supports research on skill adoption curves, linguistic evolution of instruction text, cross-tool format standardization, reuse concentration, natural language software metrics, and supply-chain security risks in the skill ecosystem.","reading_guide":"This is a dataset paper suitable for skimming to build awareness. models of different sizes based on difficulty without improving the small model's intrinsic capability, capping potential cost savings, while directly adjusting serving policies with raw online traces carries risks of silent failures and capability regression.","importance":"It proposes a closed-loop mechanism that converts online agent execution traces into signals for iterative small-model improvement instead of relying solely on routing scheduling, and its joint evolution framework of routing, skill distillation, and model adaptation provides practical reference for continuous post-training and deployment optimization of small models in production agent systems.","technical_approach":"Takes individual model invocations as the adaptation unit and splits the system into online serving and offline update pipelines. The online path uses an input-only router to select among strong, cheap, and specialized models, pairs with a SkillBook to dispatch reusable templates for recurring structured steps such as formatting and tool argument construction, and triggers fallback to stronger models plus event logging when verification fails. The offline update cycle follows a dependency-respecting Skill → LLM → Router schedule: first update SkillBook statistics for recurring patterns, then distill the small model using teacher demonstrations from failed invocations paired with the current SkillBook, finally train the router on actual small-model outcomes from the current cycle. All updates are admitted only after joint replay verification to avoid end-to-end degradation despite isolated component improvements.","experiments":"On 582 held-out HumanEval++MBPP tasks (3 seeds), 4 adaptation cycles lift Qwen2.5-Coder-1.5B accuracy from 28.7% to 44.2% with SFT, and to 49.7% with combined SFT+GRPO. On TAU-2, adapted Qwen3.5-2B improves from 14/35 to 18/35, matching the performance of an unadapted 4B endpoint. A financial break-even analysis quantifies the tradeoff between adaptation cost and serving savings.","reading_guide":"Skim the 2nd-3rd paragraphs of Introduction to understand the capability ceiling of existing routing methods, then Algorithm 1 in Method for the update cycle order and dependency logic, finally the cost-accuracy tradeoff results in Experiments. Therefore, we conduct a","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-11T10:00:42Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2608_10743.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究多轮搜索 Agent 中的 context interference：检索系统通常能召回含有答案的文档，但上下文里混入的无关文档、历史查询和中间推理会干扰模型选取正确证据，导致“召回到了但答不对”。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接贡献有限，更偏向 Agent/RAG 训练范式。但它对长上下文使用方式有启发：上下文长度增加不等于有效信息增加，噪声文档会改变模型对内部知识和外部证据的利用方式。对做 post-train、RL search agent、长上下文数据构造的人更有参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文先把搜索 Agent 的上下文拆成问题、历史搜索 query、历史检索文档、最新检索文档、历史 reasoning 等部分，通过 masking 不同上下文片段来定位干扰源。实验结论是最新一轮检索文档是主要干扰来源，历史 query 和历史文档也有轻微影响。基于这个观察，作者构造了一个 context refinement 数据集，用蒸馏方式把“检索文档”压缩为“对当前搜索 query 最关键的信息”。随后训练一个 context refiner，在 Agent 生成前动态重写检索上下文。最后把 refiner 放入 RL 训练流程，让策略模型在训练时看到的是动态精炼后的上下文，而不是原始检索结果。","experiments":"论文在一组 closed-book QA benchmark 上评估多轮搜索 Agent 的可靠性和效率。关键实验包括：不同上下文片段 masking 的归因实验；多种上下文精炼策略比较；蒸馏式 context refiner 的效果；以及把 context refinement 接入 RL search-agent training pipeline 后与其他训练基线比较。提供内容中没有给出具体 benchmark 名称、模型规模、数据规模和提升数字，因此不宜补充数值。可确认的实验结论是：refiner 同时提升答题可靠性和搜索效率；在 RL 训练中使用动态精炼上下文，比仅在推理时做上下文处理更稳定。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，约 20–30 分钟。重点看三处：第一，RQ1 的 context masking 设计，它决定了“干扰来自哪里”的可信度；第二，context refinement 数据集如何蒸馏，特别是 teacher 信号来自哪里、是否会引入答案泄漏；第三，RL pipeline 中 refiner 的接入方式，关注训练时和推理时上下文分布是否一致。对基座预训练研究员来说，不必细读具体 QA 结果，值得关注的是长上下文噪声如何影响 search-agent 训练信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies context interference in multi-turn search agents. A retriever may return documents that contain the needed evidence, but irrelevant documents, past queries, and intermediate reasoning can distract the model, creating a gap between retrieval recall and answer accuracy.","importance":"The work is not a core pretraining paper. Its relevance is mainly at the pretrain–posttrain interface: it shows that longer retrieved context can degrade the learning and inference signal for search agents. This matters for RL-trained retrieval agents, long-context training data design, and systems that mix parametric knowledge with external evidence.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose the agent context into the user question, previous search queries, previous retrieved documents, the latest retrieved documents, and reasoning history. They then mask specific context components to identify where interference comes from. Their main finding is that the latest retrieved documents are the dominant source of interference, while earlier queries and documents contribute mildly. Based on this, they build a distilled context-refinement dataset that maps retrieved documents into concise query-relevant evidence. A context refiner is trained on this dataset and inserted before generation. The refiner is also integrated into the reinforcement-learning pipeline, so the search agent is trained with dynamically refined contexts rather than raw retrieval outputs.","experiments":"The paper evaluates reliability and efficiency on several closed-book QA benchmarks. The main experiments include context-component masking, comparison among refinement strategies, evaluation of the distilled refiner, and RL training with dynamic context refinement. The supplied full text does not include exact model sizes, dataset sizes, benchmark names, or numerical gains, so those numbers should not be inferred. The reported qualitative result is consistent: context refinement improves both answer reliability and search efficiency, and using refinement during RL training is stronger than treating it only as an inference-time wrapper.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. Focus on three parts: the masking-based attribution setup for RQ1, the construction of the distilled context-refinement data, and the way the refiner is inserted into the RL training loop. The most useful question to ask while reading is whether the refiner changes the training distribution in a controlled way or simply filters away hard negatives.
